| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:137bab21-9ecd-4303-a4f8-25a1a4251bc1 | q ratio | Quality factor for compressional waves. The ratio of 2*pi times the peak compressional wave energy to the energy dissipated in a cycle. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:138018a3-ca8e-40fe-81db-40729be48f9e | min net thickness | It is common in an interbedded reservoir for sands thinner than a certain cutoff thickness (Min_Net_Thickness) to be discarded as non net or non pay in a ResSum type analysis. However, a thick sand may have two closely spaced shale stringers, such that they create the effect of a thin sand separated from thick sands above and below by thin shale beds. In this case, if the shale beds are thinner than a second thickness threshold (Min_Non_Net_Thickness), the thin sand would be counted as net or pay. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:2222e877-dde6-42ca-be3b-a4a2dead7ffb | elastic impedance | Property which controls the propagation of a seismic wave at a particular (non-zero) angle of incidence. | partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:689aa900-754f-45eb-8e25-e215844f5127 | min non net thickness | It is common in an interbedded reservoir for sands thinner than a certain cutoff thickness (Min_Net_Thickness) to be discarded as non net or non pay in a ResSum type analysis. However, a thick sand may have two closely spaced shale stringers, such that they create the effect of a thin sand separated from thick sands above and below by thin shale beds. In this case, if the shale beds are thinner than a second thickness threshold (Min_Non_Net_Thickness), the thin sand would be counted as net or pay. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:a0370748-6efb-41e7-a266-78003a27b474 | steel grade | Grade used to qualify the characteristics of the steel. For tubulars, grade determines the minimum yield strength and thus the burst and collapse pressures and is of importance for the proper choice of slips. The grade can be either custom, i.e. defined by the supplier, e.g. US Steel FS80, Vallourec 110 VS 22, or defined by the API, e.g. C75, J55, N80, E. Number in the API grade means the minimum yield strength in kpsi (N80: 80,000 psi). API grades for Tubing, Casing and Drill pipes can be found in the API specifications 5xx. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:b73688cd-bad7-4899-8b54-21adc16c3657 | amplitude - envelope | The trace envelope is a geophysical attribute, and is a representation of the amplitude of an oscillatory function. It varies approximately between 0 and the absolute value of the maximum amplitude of the trace. The envelope of seismic signals is independent of the phase of the data and may represent the individual interface contrast or, the combined response of several interfaces, depending on the seismic bandwidth. See https://wiki.seg.org/wiki/Dictionary:Trace_envelope | Value added. |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:d59e8810-fdcf-451e-91b6-b274e3cddfdf | beaufort scale number | The Beaufort wind scale is a system used to estimate and report wind speeds when no measuring apparatus is available. It was invented in the early 19th century by Admiral Sir Francis Beaufort of the British Navy as a way to interpret winds from conditions at sea, e.g. 5 on the Beaufort scale corresponds to a wind of 17-21 knots and is defined as "Fresh Breeze: Moderate waves (1.8m), many white horses." | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision, ParentName, ParentPropertyTypeID |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:e48b1197-6753-4628-9013-76165b02db68 | bandwidth rating bias | Statistical estimation of bandwidth of data within window. Aids in determining appropriate spectral operators for subsequent operations. Computation: Let the autocorrelation of the trace be A[i]. Then compute M = max(A) and S = sum(A[i]**2). The bandwidth rating bias B is then : B = M**2 / r(2 S - M**2), where r is the sample rate. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |

| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ff9f0071-6e82-454f-9f91-46229d175f5e | volume per geologic time | Volume per geologic time. E.g. which volume of hydrocarbons were generated in a particular time frame | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ffa19a04-b624-41b9-83dd-eeab20241780 | affine coeff b | Coefficient "B" in an Affine transformation between two coordinate systems. The input and output coordinate systems may be local or projection systems. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ffa5f462-4c56-4699-8cff-a212eb08a5c5 | sediment and water | Measure of impurities which are contained in oil produced from a well, as described in ASTM Standard Test D96-82. Traditionally called Basic Sediment and Water. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ffb36810-1c71-41f3-a049-57ac5e576042 | max allowable pressure | Maximum allowable pressure for the equipment | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ffb3d980-2238-4e60-b22b-668fa7f9881d | poisson ratio | Ratio of the transverse contraction of a body to its longitudinal extension when stretched. Typical values in minerals are from 0.15 to 0.3 (Carbonates:0.15, Sandstones:0.2, Shales:0.25). | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:ffe73807-8a7f-43cd-93e3-625d78f480d2 | apparent water resistivity | Apparent resistivity of connate (formation) water, generally from logs; such estimates require correction before they can be used quantitatively. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:fff5ba6c-deb7-4eff-98b0-e179408bc5e8 | hole inside diameter | The effective inside diameter of a Well; may be of the Borehole, Casing or Tubing. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:fff648f9-93d6-4480-9a8a-17c91003c9f6 | max surge pressure | Maximum pressure to which this Equipment can be exposed for a short time without permanent damage; the Equipment will again perform to specifications when the pressure is reduced below the maximum working pressure. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
| partition-id:reference-data--PropertyType:fffecd9b-a7f7-4fa7-be45-71d70e38b731 | washout caliper ext | The extension of a borehole diameter beyond which caliper data becomes unreliable due to bad contact with the tool pads. | Changes in AttributionAuthority, AttributionRevision |
